build_reconstruction.py
This helper has not been executed. It is included to make the delivered text reconstructible when a filesystem and TeX runtime are available; it does not stand in for a successful build.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rebuild the chat-delivered reconstruction. No network access.

Requirements: Python 3, Node.js, LuaLaTeX, BibTeX, and the TeX
packages named in LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_paper.tex.

This helper was supplied after the workspace outage and was not run
in the research session. It preserves the supplied stdout certificates.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import csv
import re
import subprocess
import zipfile

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MAIN = ROOT / "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_paper.tex"
BIB = ROOT / "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_references.bib"
SECTIONS = [
    ROOT / "sections" / name
    for name in ["00_conventions.tex", "A.tex", "B.tex", "C.tex",
                 "D.tex", "E.tex", "F.tex"]
]
PROGRAM_NAMES = [
    "ab_reconstructed",
    "c_replacement",
    "d_reconstructed",
    "e_arithmetic_replacement",
    "e_groups_replacement",
    "e_fermat",
]
PROGRAMS = [ROOT / "code" / (name + ".js") for name in PROGRAM_NAMES]
OUTPUTS = [ROOT / "code" / (name + ".out") for name in PROGRAM_NAMES]
REQUIRED = [
    MAIN, BIB, ROOT / "results.csv", ROOT / "readings.csv",
    ROOT / "report.md", ROOT / "code" / "e_additional.py",
    *SECTIONS, *PROGRAMS, *OUTPUTS,
]
missing = [str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) for p in REQUIRED if not p.is_file()]
if missing:
    raise SystemExit("Missing delivered inputs:\n" + "\n".join(missing))

# Explicitly disclosed transcription repairs; no mathematical source
# or bibliographic content is silently invented.
repairs = {
    ROOT / "sections" / "A.tex": [
        ("Its unique fixed line is",
         "Its unique line on which the operator acts pointwise as the identity is")
    ],
    ROOT / "sections" / "F.tex": [
        (r"$(\mathbb Z/n)^r and", r"$(\mathbb Z/n)^r$ and")
    ],
    ROOT / "code" / "d_reconstructed.js": [
        ("p[0]===a&&p[1]===b&&p[1]===b",
         "p[0]===a&&p[1]===b")
    ],
}
for path, replacements in repairs.items():
    old = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    new = old
    for before, after in replacements:
        new = new.replace(before, after)
    if new != old:
        path.write_text(new, encoding="utf-8")

with (ROOT / "results.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as stream:
    rows = list(csv.DictReader(stream))
expected = {
    f"{part}.{i}"
    for part, count in [("A", 11), ("B", 23), ("C", 11),
                        ("D", 21), ("E", 28), ("F", 3)]
    for i in range(1, count + 1)
}
ids = [row["id"] for row in rows]
if len(ids) != 97 or len(set(ids)) != 97 or set(ids) != expected:
    raise SystemExit("The statement register must contain exactly the 97 expected IDs.")
allowed = {"Proved", "Refuted", "Known", "Open", "Unfinished"}
if any(row["status"] not in allowed for row in rows):
    raise SystemExit("Unrecognized statement status.")

with (ROOT / "readings.csv").open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as stream:
    readings = list(csv.DictReader(stream))
if len(readings) != 57 or len({r["id"] for r in readings}) != 57:
    raise SystemExit("The reconstructed readings register must have 57 unique rows.")

tex = "\n".join(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8") for p in [MAIN, *SECTIONS])
paper_ids = re.findall(r"\\begin\{statement\}\{([^}]+)\}", tex)
if len(paper_ids) != 97 or set(paper_ids) != expected:
    raise SystemExit("The paper's numbered statements do not match the register.")
cited = set()
for match in re.finditer(
    r"\\cite[a-zA-Z]*\s*(?:
$$
[^
$$
]*\]\s*){0,2}\{([^}]*)\}", tex
):
    cited.update(k.strip() for k in match.group(1).split(",") if k.strip())
bib_keys = set(re.findall(
    r"@\w+\s*\{\s*([^,\s]+)", BIB.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
))
unresolved = sorted(cited - bib_keys)
if unresolved:
    raise SystemExit(
        "Unresolved citation keys; recover or verify their actual sources "
        "before continuing:\n" + "\n".join(unresolved)
    )

LOGS = ROOT / "build"
LOGS.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

def run(command, log_name):
    completed = subprocess.run(
        command, cwd=ROOT, text=True,
        stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
        check=False,
    )
    (LOGS / log_name).write_text(completed.stdout, encoding="utf-8")
    if completed.returncode:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"Command failed with code {completed.returncode}: {command}\n"
            f"See build/{log_name}"
        )
    return completed.stdout

for program, expected_output in zip(PROGRAMS, OUTPUTS):
    actual = run(["node", str(program)], program.stem + ".rerun.out")
    supplied = expected_output.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if actual.strip() != supplied.strip():
        raise SystemExit(
            f"Output mismatch for {program.name}; "
            "the supplied certificate has not been overwritten."
        )

stem = MAIN.stem
run(["lualatex", "-interaction=nonstopmode", "-halt-on-error", MAIN.name],
    "lualatex-pass1.txt")
run(["bibtex", stem], "bibtex.txt")
run(["lualatex", "-interaction=nonstopmode", "-halt-on-error", MAIN.name],
    "lualatex-pass2.txt")
run(["lualatex", "-interaction=nonstopmode", "-halt-on-error", MAIN.name],
    "lualatex-pass3.txt")
final_log = (ROOT / (stem + ".log")).read_text(
    encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"
)
if "There were undefined references" in final_log or "Citation" in final_log and "undefined" in final_log:
    raise SystemExit("The final TeX log still contains unresolved references or citations.")

PDF = ROOT / (stem + ".pdf")
if not PDF.is_file() or not PDF.read_bytes().startswith(b"%PDF-"):
    raise SystemExit("No valid PDF header was produced.")

ARCHIVE = ROOT / "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_reconstruction.zip"
members = [*REQUIRED, Path(__file__).resolve(), PDF, *sorted(LOGS.iterdir())]
with zipfile.ZipFile(ARCHIVE, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
    for path in members:
        archive.write(path, path.relative_to(ROOT))

print("Compiled the reconstruction and matched all six JavaScript outputs.")
print("Statement rows: 97. Reading rows: 57.")
print("Archive:", ARCHIVE.name)
print("Visual PDF review has not been performed by this helper.")
print("The report's mathematical and recovery limitations remain in force.")

sections/C12_addendum.tex
\subsection*{Addendum: the projective image over
\texorpdfstring{$\mathbb Z/4$}{Z/4}}

\begin{statement}{C.12}{Proved}
{The remaining finite projective image has order twenty-four}
Let $R=\mathbb Z/4$. Then

$$
\operatorname{im}\bigl(A_p(R)\longrightarrow\operatorname{PGL}_2(R)\bigr)
 =
 \operatorname{im}\bigl(\operatorname{SL}_2(R)
                  \longrightarrow\operatorname{PGL}_2(R)\bigr).
$$

This image has order $24$. Its complement is exactly the $24$
projective classes of nonsquare determinant listed in the
$R=\mathbb Z/4$ row of the replacement computation. In particular
$[\operatorname{diag}(3,1)]$ does not lift through $A_p(R)$.
This settles the finite image question left open in C.11.
The other source-section and integral-point questions stated there
are not settled by this addendum.
\end{statement}

\begin{proof}
We use the big fppf sheaf, which can be evaluated on arbitrary
$R$-algebras. In particular a localization not finitely presented
over $R$ is a legitimate object on which to pull back a section;
it is not thereby asserted to be an fppf cover.

\emph{The source on flat objects.}
Write $t=u(1)$ in
$\Gamma_p=\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb Z)$.
Since $4=0$ in every additive $R$-group, the pushout defining $A_p$
factors the constant group through

$$
H=\Gamma_p/\langle\!\langle t^4\rangle\!\rangle
  =\operatorname{SL}_2(R)/\{\pm I\}.
$$

The last equality, including order $24$, is C.7. Its cusp
$C=\langle t\rangle$ has order four. The universal property of
pushouts therefore identifies the base-changed source with
$\underline H*_{\underline C}\mathbb G_a$.

Restricting to flat $R$-objects commutes with sheafification:
every fppf cover of a flat $R$-object still consists of flat
$R$-objects, so the same local constructions compute the restriction.
On this restricted site $\underline C\to\mathbb G_a$ is a
monomorphism. Indeed every nonzero flat algebra over the Artinian
local ring $R$ is faithfully flat. The same applies to any nonzero
clopen factor, so distinct locally constant residues modulo four
cannot acquire the same additive value. This assertion is not used
on the nonflat $R$-object $\mathbb F_2$.

\emph{Local preservation of ordinary normal forms.}
For every nonzero local flat $R$-algebra $S$, put
$W(S)=H*_C(S,+)$. The natural map $W(S)\to A_p(S)$ is injective.
To prove this, suppose an ordinary reduced word becomes the identity
in the sheaf. Equality in sheafification is witnessed locally by
equality in the presheaf, after refining a cover if necessary.
Choose a covering point above the closed point of
$\operatorname{Spec}S$ and localize its covering algebra there.
The resulting local map $S\to S'$ is flat and hence faithfully flat
\citep[Lemma~10.39.17, Tag~00HR]{C_Stacks_00HR}.
In particular it is injective. Since both rings are local, their
constant $H$ and $C$ sections are exactly $H$ and $C$.
An additive syllable outside $C$ cannot become an element of $C$
in $S'$, and the constant syllables do not change. The ordinary
amalgam normal-form theorem gives a contradiction.

\emph{A fixed reduced word on an Artinian local refinement.}
Take $\alpha\in A_p(R)$, and let
$g\in\operatorname{PGL}_2(R)$ be its matrix image.
Choose a nonempty affine fppf cover
$\operatorname{Spec}B\to\operatorname{Spec}R$ on which $\alpha$
is represented by a finite word. We may choose $B$ finitely
presented over $R$, so it is Noetherian. Let $P$ be a minimal prime
of $B$ and put $B_0=B_P$. This is a nonzero Noetherian local ring
of dimension zero, hence Artinian. It is flat and faithfully flat
over $R$. The map
$\operatorname{Spec}B_0\to\operatorname{Spec}R$
is an affine fpqc cover; it need not be fppf.

Reduce the pulled-back word in $W(B_0)$. If it is the identity,
then $g=1$ by fpqc separatedness of the representable target.
Otherwise write it as $x_1\cdots x_m$, with fixed alternating
factor types. For $m\geq2$ every syllable lies outside $C$.
For $m=1$ the subsequent argument uses the trivial insertion group.

Set $D=B_0\otimes_R B_0$. For every prime $q$ of $D$, the ring
$D_q$ is nonzero and flat over either copy of $B_0$.
Because $B_0$ is Artinian local, each map $B_0\to D_q$ is local,
and is therefore faithfully flat by Tag~00HR. Equivalently one
may use flatness over an Artinian local ring
\citep[Lemma~10.101.2, Tag~051G]{C_Stacks_051G}.
Consequently both pullback words retain the same reduced length
and factor types over $D_q$. Their products are equal in $A_p(D_q)$,
since both come from $\alpha$. The local injectivity just proved
makes them equal as ordinary reduced words.

\emph{Unique finite insertions and their descent.}
Equality of reduced words of these fixed types is equivalent to
unique elements $c_1,\ldots,c_{m-1}\in C$ satisfying

$$
y_1=x_1c_1,\qquad
 y_i=c_{i-1}^{-1}x_i c_i\quad(1<i<m),\qquad
 y_m=c_{m-1}^{-1}x_m.
$$

For clarity, uniqueness and existence follow by cancellation:
$x_1^{-1}y_1$ must lie in $C$, or the full inverse-product word
would remain reduced; this determines $c_1$, and induction gives
the rest. At each $D_q$ the inserted elements are ordinary
constant residues. There are finitely many additive equalities
in the displayed formulas. Each equality holding in $D_q$ holds
on some principal open neighbourhood of $q$. The constant-group
equalities hold there too. Thus the insertion tuples exist
Zariski locally on $\operatorname{Spec}D$.

Let $G=\underline C^{\,m-1}$ and let $X$ be the product of the
$m$ indicated factors, each either $\underline H$ or
$\mathbb G_a$. This is an affine $R$-scheme. There is a right action

$$
(x_1,\ldots,x_m)\cdot(c_1,\ldots,c_{m-1})
 =
 (x_1c_1,c_1^{-1}x_2c_2,\ldots,c_{m-1}^{-1}x_m).
$$

It is free on flat $R$-objects: equality of first coordinates
forces $c_1=1$, and induction forces every $c_i=1$, using the
monomorphism $C\to\mathbb G_a$ only on flat objects.
Therefore the local insertion tuples agree on intersections and
glue to $c\in G(D)$. On the triple overlap, freeness gives
$c_{13}=c_{12}c_{23}$.

No globally decidable word-length stratum has been introduced:
the fixed word was obtained over $B_0$, its reducedness was
checked in the local rings of overlaps, and the finite equalities
were then spread to open neighbourhoods.

The cocycle descends the trivial $G$-torsor over $B_0$ to a finite
\'etale $G$-torsor $T\to\operatorname{Spec}R$.
Effective descent of affine morphisms is
\citet[Lemma~35.37.1, Tag~0245]{C_Stacks_0245};
finite locally free algebras descend by faithfully flat module
descent, and \'etaleness descends by
\citet[Lemma~35.23.31, Tag~02VN]{C_Stacks_02VN}.
The tuple $(x_1,\ldots,x_m)$ gives a compatible equivariant map
from that trivial torsor to $X$, so it descends to a morphism
$f:T\to X$. Equivalently, $f$ is a tuple of ordinary word
coordinates over the finite \'etale cover $T$.

\emph{Only the matrix image is descended back.}
Let $\theta:X\to\operatorname{PGL}_2$ multiply the matrix images
of the coordinates. Cusp insertions cancel, so $\theta$ is
$G$-invariant. Consequently $\theta\circ f$ agrees with $g_T$
after the faithfully flat pullback
$B_0\otimes_R\mathcal O(T)$.
Since scheme-representable functors are fpqc sheaves
\citep[Lemma~35.13.7, Tag~023Q]{C_Stacks_023Q},
the two maps agree already over $T$.

Each $H$-coordinate has a determinant-one matrix representative
over $R$; choosing one for each of the finitely many elements of
$H$ also gives representatives for locally constant
$H$-coordinates over $T$. The additive coordinates have their
elementary determinant-one representatives. Their product is
therefore an element of $\operatorname{SL}_2(T)$ representing
$g_T$.

This conclusion uses fpqc descent for the affine coordinates and
the matrix target. It does not claim fpqc descent for $A_p$, nor
that $\alpha_T$ itself has been identified with the descended
word inside $A_p(T)$.

\emph{The determinant obstruction survives every such cover.}
The element $3$ is not a square in any nonzero finite \'etale
$R$-algebra $T$. Indeed $T/2T$ is reduced. If $z^2=3$, then
$(\overline z-1)^2=0$, so $\overline z=1$.
Hence $z=1+2b$ for some $b\in T$, and $z^2=1$, a contradiction
to $3\ne1$ in the faithfully flat algebra $T$.

If $g=[\operatorname{diag}(3,1)]$ had arisen from $A_p(R)$,
the preceding construction would give a determinant-one matrix
representing it over $T$. Equality of two matrix representatives
in $\operatorname{PGL}_2(T)$ means that their ratio is a global
scalar unit. Taking determinants would make $3$ a square in $T$,
which is impossible.

Finally C.10 already supplies the whole projective special-linear
subgroup, of order $24$ and index two. Any additional image
element would yield the excluded class after multiplication by
an element of that subgroup. Thus the image is exactly the
stated subgroup.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Effect on the earlier residual.}
In C.11, the uncertainty about the order of the projective image
over $\mathbb Z/4$ and the lifting of
$[\operatorname{diag}(3,1)]$ is superseded by C.12:
that class does not lift, and the image has order $24$.
The computed list of $24$ missing word-image classes is now also
the complete list of missing sheaf-image classes.
The countably infinite source point group in C.8 is unchanged.
Complete source-group descriptions and the stated integral-point
questions remain outside this addendum.

Constructing helper code

RECOVERY_UPDATE.md
# Recovery update: C.12 and the additional Python output

This update supplements the complete text-file edition already delivered.
It does not claim that the full paper has been compiled or that a final
PDF or ZIP has been made available.

## Mathematical amendment

C.12 proves

    im(A_p(Z/4) -> PGL2(Z/4)) = PSL2(Z/4),

with image order 24. The class [diag(3,1)] does not lift. The 24
nonsquare-determinant classes already enumerated by the computation are
exactly the missing points of the full sheaf image.

The proof uses the big fppf site. An Artinian local fpqc refinement
preserves reduced words in local rings of overlaps. Unique constant-C4
insertions descend a finite etale torsor and affine word coordinates.
Equality is descended in the representable matrix target. No fpqc sheaf
property for the amalgam is assumed.

This resolves the Z/4 image uncertainty in the earlier C.11 discussion.
It does not change the proved countable infinitude of A_p(Z/4).

C.11 remains Open in the register for the following residual:
full abstract descriptions of the descended Artinian source groups, and
the comparison of A_s(Z) and A_p(Z) with their ordinary integral word
subgroups, have not been proved here.

The Part C.d and Item 2 outcomes remain partly settled for those broader
questions. The Z/4 image question must be removed from their residual
lists. C.10's square-determinant image conclusions now extend to Z/4 by
C.12.

The earlier program output's “24 or 48” limitation remains an authentic
record of what that bounded search alone established. It is superseded
mathematically by C.12 and must not be reported as the final uncertainty.

## Final statement register

The integrated edition has 98 numbered statements:

| Status | Count |
|---|---:|
| Proved | 72 |
| Refuted | 16 |
| Known | 4 |
| Open | 5 |
| Unfinished | 1 |
| Total | 98 |

The five Open entries are C.11, D.8, E.11, E.21, and E.27.
A.10 remains Unfinished.

These are statuses of this investigation. An Open entry does not by
itself assert that the corresponding question is open in the literature.

The manuscript-reading register remains at 57 rows. C.12 adds no new
manuscript transcription or pixel-reading claim. Five checked Stacks
Project references supplement the bibliography.

## Fresh execution evidence

The retained source code/e_additional.py was rerun under Python with
SymPy in the recovered execution service. It exited with status 0;
standard error was empty. Its complete fresh stdout is supplied as
code/e_additional.out: 1,723 ASCII/UTF-8 bytes including the final newline.

This is newly executed output. It is not a purported recovery of the
inaccessible original stdout bytes.

All three Part E JavaScript programs also ran successfully under Node.
Their outputs matched the retained outputs, allowing only terminal
newline normalization. The Part D JavaScript rerun reproduced its
retained output byte for byte, including its final newline.

The six replacement JavaScript sources and their previously delivered
actual outputs remain part of the edition. Together with the recovered
Python source and its fresh output, they provide seven executable
computation files with actual outputs.

The older inaccessible Python sources and ancillary files listed in
report.md have not thereby been recovered. Their provenance limitations
remain in force.

## Execution and delivery limits

The original workspace directory was absent in the restarted service.
Ordinary execution calls did not preserve newly written files across
calls. A continuing shell retained its own files, but those files were
not visible to the separate file-viewing tool.

The full paper source already delivered in chat remains the authoritative
reconstruction. Reconstructing it again from compressed summaries would
not preserve its exact wording.

No completed full-paper LuaLaTeX build, full-paper visual validation,
final PDF, or final ZIP is claimed. The earlier D-only temporary build
does not validate this complete edition.

## Integration

Place these additions beside the files already delivered:

- sections/C12_addendum.tex
- evidence/C12_references.bib
- evidence/C12_result.json
- code/e_additional.out
- RECOVERY_UPDATE.md
- integrate_and_build_recovery.py

The new helper integrates C.12 into the main LaTeX input, appends its
references to the main bibliography, updates C.11 and C.10 metadata,
and inserts C.12 into results.csv. It places this update after the
initial outcome table in report.md, preserving the requirement that
the report begin with all task, Item, Lead, and followup outcome rows.

The helper also applies the three previously disclosed transcription
repairs: A.2's pointwise-identity wording, F.1's missing math delimiter,
and the redundant repeated predicate in the D program.

The helper is supplied as unexecuted source. It has not been run against
the complete recovered paper. Its --build option checks statement and
reading registers, reruns all seven computations against the supplied
outputs, runs LuaLaTeX/BibTeX, and creates an archive only after these
checks succeed. Its --render option produces page images for subsequent
visual inspection; rendering alone does not constitute visual approval.

Use this helper for the 98-statement edition. The earlier
build_reconstruction.py belongs to the 97-statement edition and is
retained only as part of the recovery history.
integrate_and_build_recovery.py
This is a complete unexecuted integration and build helper. It makes no claim that the reconstructed full paper has passed compilation or visual inspection.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Integrate the recovery addendum; optionally verify, build, and archive.

Usage:
    python3 integrate_and_build_recovery.py
    python3 integrate_and_build_recovery.py --build
    python3 integrate_and_build_recovery.py --build --render

Run from the reconstructed edition's root, or pass --root PATH.
This helper was supplied as source and was not executed against the
complete edition during the conversation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import collections
import csv
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import zipfile


COUNTS = {"A": 11, "B": 23, "C": 12, "D": 21, "E": 28, "F": 3}
EXPECTED_IDS = {
    f"{part}.{number}"
    for part, count in COUNTS.items()
    for number in range(1, count + 1)
}
EXPECTED_STATUSES = {
    "proved": 72,
    "refuted": 16,
    "known": 4,
    "open": 5,
    "unfinished": 1,
}
PROGRAMS = [
    ("node", "ab_reconstructed", "js"),
    ("node", "c_replacement", "js"),
    ("node", "d_reconstructed", "js"),
    ("node", "e_arithmetic_replacement", "js"),
    ("node", "e_groups_replacement", "js"),
    ("node", "e_fermat", "js"),
    ("python", "e_additional", "py"),
]
MAIN = "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_paper"
UPDATE_START = "<!-- BEGIN RECOVERY UPDATE C12 -->"
UPDATE_END = "<!-- END RECOVERY UPDATE C12 -->"


def require(condition, message):
    if not condition:
        raise RuntimeError(message)


def normalize_id(value):
    match = re.fullmatch(r"([A-F])\.?(\d+)", str(value).strip())
    require(match is not None, f"Invalid statement ID: {value!r}")
    return f"{match.group(1)}.{int(match.group(2))}"


def read_text(path):
    require(path.is_file(), f"Missing required file: {path}")
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def write_changed(root, path, value):
    old = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if path.exists() else None
    if old == value:
        return
    if old is not None:
        backup = root / "build" / "pre_addendum" / path.relative_to(root)
        backup.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if not backup.exists():
            backup.write_text(old, encoding="utf-8")
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(value, encoding="utf-8")


def read_csv(path):
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8", newline="") as handle:
        reader = csv.DictReader(handle)
        fields = reader.fieldnames
        rows = list(reader)
    require(fields is not None, f"Missing CSV header: {path}")
    return fields, rows


def csv_text(fields, rows):
    stream = io.StringIO(newline="")
    writer = csv.DictWriter(stream, fieldnames=fields, lineterminator="\n")
    writer.writeheader()
    writer.writerows(rows)
    return stream.getvalue()


def cell(value):
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return "; ".join(str(item) for item in value)
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "true" if value else "false"
    return str(value)


def bib_keys(text):
    return set(re.findall(r"@\w+\s*\{\s*([^,\s]+)\s*,", text))


def integrate(root):
    addendum_path = root / "sections" / "C12_addendum.tex"
    addendum = read_text(addendum_path)
    require(
        re.search(r"\\begin\{statement\}\{C\.12\}\{Proved\}", addendum),
        "The addendum must contain the proved C.12 statement.",
    )

    main_path = root / f"{MAIN}.tex"
    main_text = read_text(main_path)
    new_input = r"\input{sections/C12_addendum.tex}"
    if new_input not in main_text:
        anchor = re.search(r"\\input\{sections/C(?:\.tex)?\}", main_text)
        require(anchor is not None, "Cannot locate the Part C input.")
        main_text = (
            main_text[:anchor.end()]
            + "\n"
            + new_input
            + main_text[anchor.end():]
        )
        write_changed(root, main_path, main_text)

    bibliography_path = root / "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_references.bib"
    bibliography = read_text(bibliography_path)
    extra_bibliography = read_text(root / "evidence" / "C12_references.bib")
    existing = bib_keys(bibliography)
    additional = bib_keys(extra_bibliography)
    require(len(additional) == 5, "Expected five addendum bibliography keys.")
    if not additional.issubset(existing):
        require(
            not (additional & existing),
            "Only part of the addendum bibliography is present; review manually.",
        )
        bibliography += (
            "\n\n% C12 recovery addendum references\n"
            + extra_bibliography.rstrip()
            + "\n"
        )
        write_changed(root, bibliography_path, bibliography)

    result_path = root / "results.csv"
    fields, rows = read_csv(result_path)
    required_fields = {
        "id", "statement", "status", "where_proved", "depends_on",
        "manuscript_passages", "depends_on_doubtful_reading", "notes",
    }
    require(
        required_fields.issubset(fields),
        "results.csv does not have the expected metadata columns.",
    )
    result = json.loads(read_text(root / "evidence" / "C12_result.json"))
    require(normalize_id(result["id"]) == "C.12", "Wrong addendum result ID.")
    new_row = {field: cell(result.get(field, "")) for field in fields}
    new_row["id"] = "C.12"
    new_row["status"] = "Proved"

    updated = []
    found_c11 = False
    for row in rows:
        statement_id = normalize_id(row["id"])
        if statement_id == "C.12":
            continue
        if statement_id == "C.10":
            note = " C.12 extends the square-determinant image conclusion to Z/4."
            if note.strip() not in row["notes"]:
                row["notes"] += note
        if statement_id == "C.11":
            found_c11 = True
            row["statement"] = (
                "Full abstract descriptions of the descended Artinian source "
                "groups, and comparison of A_s(Z), A_p(Z) with their ordinary "
                "integral word subgroups, remain unproved here. The finite "
                "Z/4 image question formerly included here is resolved by C.12."
            )
            row["status"] = "Open"
            row["where_proved"] = (
                "C.11: remaining source-section questions; "
                "C.12: the settled Z/4 projective image"
            )
            if not re.search(r"\bC\.?12\b", row["depends_on"]):
                row["depends_on"] = (
                    row["depends_on"].rstrip("; ") + "; C.12"
                ).lstrip("; ")
            row["notes"] = (
                "C.12 proves image PSL2(Z/4), order 24, and excludes "
                "[diag(3,1)]. Keep the broader source-section and integral "
                "comparison questions unresolved. The older bounded-search "
                "output is preserved as historical computation evidence."
            )
        updated.append(row)
        if statement_id == "C.11":
            updated.append(new_row)
    require(found_c11, "Cannot find the existing C.11 result.")
    write_changed(root, result_path, csv_text(fields, updated))

    repairs = [
        (
            root / "sections" / "A.tex",
            "Its unique fixed line is",
            "Its unique line on which the operator acts pointwise "
            "as the identity is",
        ),
        (
            root / "sections" / "F.tex",
            r"indexed by $(\mathbb Z/n)^r and",
            r"indexed by $(\mathbb Z/n)^r$ and",
        ),
        (
            root / "code" / "d_reconstructed.js",
            "p[0]===a&&p[1]===b&&p[1]===b",
            "p[0]===a&&p[1]===b",
        ),
    ]
    for path, old, new in repairs:
        contents = read_text(path)
        if old in contents:
            write_changed(root, path, contents.replace(old, new))

    report_path = root / "report.md"
    report = read_text(report_path)
    report = re.sub(
        re.escape(UPDATE_START) + r".*?" + re.escape(UPDATE_END) + r"\n*",
        "",
        report,
        flags=re.S,
    )
    lines = report.splitlines(keepends=True)
    first = next((i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.strip()), None)
    require(
        first is not None and lines[first].lstrip().startswith("|"),
        "report.md must begin with its full outcome table.",
    )
    position = first
    while position < len(lines) and lines[position].lstrip().startswith("|"):
        position += 1
    update = read_text(root / "RECOVERY_UPDATE.md").rstrip()
    insertion = (
        "\n"
        + UPDATE_START
        + "\n"
        + update
        + "\n"
        + UPDATE_END
        + "\n\n"
    )
    write_changed(
        root,
        report_path,
        "".join(lines[:position]) + insertion + "".join(lines[position:]),
    )


def verify_registers(root):
    _, results = read_csv(root / "results.csv")
    ids = [normalize_id(row["id"]) for row in results]
    require(len(ids) == len(set(ids)), "Duplicate statement IDs.")
    require(set(ids) == EXPECTED_IDS, "The result ID set is not the expected 98.")
    statuses = collections.Counter(
        row["status"].strip().lower() for row in results
    )
    require(
        dict(statuses) == EXPECTED_STATUSES,
        f"Unexpected status totals: {dict(statuses)}",
    )
    _, readings = read_csv(root / "readings.csv")
    require(len(readings) == 57, "Expected the 57-row reading register.")

    paths = [root / f"{MAIN}.tex"]
    paths += [
        root / "sections" / name
        for name in (
            "00_conventions.tex", "A.tex", "B.tex", "C.tex",
            "C12_addendum.tex", "D.tex", "E.tex", "F.tex",
        )
    ]
    combined = "\n".join(read_text(path) for path in paths)
    theorem_ids = re.findall(
        r"\\begin\{statement\}\{([A-F]\.\d+)\}", combined
    )
    require(
        len(theorem_ids) == 98 and set(theorem_ids) == EXPECTED_IDS,
        "The LaTeX statement IDs do not match results.csv.",
    )
    bibliography = read_text(root / "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_references.bib")
    cited = set()
    pattern = r"\\cite[a-zA-Z]*\*?(?:
$$
[^
$$
]*\]){0,2}\{([^}]+)\}"
    for match in re.finditer(pattern, combined):
        cited.update(key.strip() for key in match.group(1).split(","))
    missing = cited - bib_keys(bibliography)
    require(not missing, f"Missing bibliography keys: {sorted(missing)}")
    return {
        "statements": len(ids),
        "statuses": dict(statuses),
        "readings": len(readings),
        "citation_keys_checked": len(cited),
    }


def run_logged(root, command, label):
    result = subprocess.run(
        command, cwd=root, capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
    )
    log_directory = root / "build" / "verification"
    log_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (log_directory / f"{label}.stdout").write_text(
        result.stdout, encoding="utf-8"
    )
    (log_directory / f"{label}.stderr").write_text(
        result.stderr, encoding="utf-8"
    )
    require(result.returncode == 0, f"{label} failed; see its saved logs.")
    return result.stdout


def normalized_output(value):
    return value.replace("\r\n", "\n").rstrip("\n")


def build(root, render):
    verification = verify_registers(root)
    node = shutil.which("node")
    lualatex = shutil.which("lualatex")
    bibtex = shutil.which("bibtex")
    require(node and lualatex and bibtex, "Node, LuaLaTeX, and BibTeX are required.")

    computations = []
    for interpreter, stem, extension in PROGRAMS:
        source = root / "code" / f"{stem}.{extension}"
        expected = root / "code" / f"{stem}.out"
        read_text(source)
        expected_output = read_text(expected)
        executable = node if interpreter == "node" else sys.executable
        actual = run_logged(root, [executable, str(source)], stem)
        require(
            normalized_output(actual) == normalized_output(expected_output),
            f"Output mismatch for {stem}; original and fresh outputs are preserved.",
        )
        computations.append({
            "program": source.relative_to(root).as_posix(),
            "matched_supplied_output": True,
            "stdout_sha256": hashlib.sha256(actual.encode()).hexdigest(),
        })

    (root / "build").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    latex_command = [
        lualatex, "-interaction=nonstopmode", "-halt-on-error",
        "-file-line-error", "-output-directory=build", f"{MAIN}.tex",
    ]
    run_logged(root, latex_command, "lualatex-1")
    run_logged(root, [bibtex, f"build/{MAIN}"], "bibtex")
    run_logged(root, latex_command, "lualatex-2")
    run_logged(root, latex_command, "lualatex-3")

    log = read_text(root / "build" / f"{MAIN}.log")
    require(
        not re.search(
            r"undefined (?:references|citations)|"
            r"(?:Reference|Citation) .*? undefined",
            log,
            flags=re.I,
        ),
        "Unresolved references or citations remain in the LaTeX log.",
    )
    generated_pdf = root / "build" / f"{MAIN}.pdf"
    require(generated_pdf.is_file(), "LuaLaTeX produced no PDF.")
    require(
        generated_pdf.read_bytes().startswith(b"%PDF-"),
        "The generated file has no PDF header.",
    )
    final_pdf = root / f"{MAIN}.pdf"
    shutil.copyfile(generated_pdf, final_pdf)

    verification["computations"] = computations
    verification["pdf_sha256"] = hashlib.sha256(
        final_pdf.read_bytes()
    ).hexdigest()
    verification["overfull_box_warnings"] = len(
        re.findall(r"Overfull \\[hv]box", log)
    )
    verification["visual_review_performed"] = False

    if render:
        pdftoppm = shutil.which("pdftoppm")
        require(pdftoppm, "pdftoppm is required for --render.")
        page_directory = root / "build" / "pages"
        page_directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        run_logged(
            root,
            [
                pdftoppm, "-png", "-r", "100",
                str(final_pdf), str(page_directory / "page"),
            ],
            "render",
        )
        verification["rendered_page_images"] = len(
            list(page_directory.glob("page-*.png"))
        )

    verification_path = root / "build" / "verification.json"
    verification_path.write_text(
        json.dumps(verification, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )

    members = []
    for pattern in ("*.tex", "*.bib", "*.csv", "*.md", "*.py", "*.pdf"):
        members.extend(root.glob(pattern))
    for directory in ("sections", "code", "evidence"):
        members.extend(
            path for path in (root / directory).rglob("*") if path.is_file()
        )
    members.append(verification_path)
    members.extend(
        path for path in (root / "build" / "verification").rglob("*")
        if path.is_file()
    )
    members.append(root / "build" / f"{MAIN}.log")

    archive = root.parent / "LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES_reconstructed_20261111.zip"
    with zipfile.ZipFile(
        archive, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
    ) as handle:
        for path in sorted(set(members)):
            handle.write(
                path,
                (Path("LM1_GROUP_SCHEMES") / path.relative_to(root)).as_posix(),
            )

    print(json.dumps({
        "archive": str(archive),
        "pdf": str(final_pdf),
        "verification": verification,
        "notice": "Rendering is not visual review. Inspect the final PDF.",
    }, indent=2))


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--root", type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    )
    parser.add_argument("--build", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--render", action="store_true")
    arguments = parser.parse_args()
    require(not arguments.render or arguments.build, "--render requires --build.")
    root = arguments.root.resolve()
    integrate(root)
    verification = verify_registers(root)
    if arguments.build:
        build(root, arguments.render)
    else:
        print(json.dumps({
            "integration": "completed",
            "register_verification": verification,
            "full_pdf_build_performed": False,
            "visual_review_performed": False,
        }, indent=2))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    try:
        main()
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"Recovery integration/build failed: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        raise SystemExit(1)
